# Indonesia payroll obligations — working inventory

Reviewed 27 September 2026 for pay periods from **1 December 2025**. This independent Indonesia
register is not a certification. It covers
identified national instruments and local floors, including floors absent from the ID seed; the
province/regency/city and KBLI schedule, amendment history, and several worker classes remain
unreconciled. **No complete provision denominator or independent legal sign-off exists yet.**

**Verified correction — untaken rest break (shared-engine defect 2/22, 2026-09-28):** [UU 13/2003 art.79(2)(a)](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/peraturan/detail/27/undang-undang-nomor-13-tahun-2003) owes half an hour after four continuous hours and excludes that break, when taken, from working time. It is silent on a break owed and not taken. Register default (owner rule 2026-09-28): the minutes worked through it are worked time and pay; the run reports the missed break (`restBreak`) and deducts nothing. The golden harness had deducted the shortfall on rest days and holidays, which production never did; its goldens now price every clocked minute.

## Scope and source ledger

The ID seed has three active sealed periods: 1 December 2025–1 January 2026,
1 January–1 March 2026, and 1 March 2026 onward. The governing row and its dated child rows
are in [`seed/jurisdiction/ID/`](../../seed/jurisdiction/ID/). This does not mean every local
floor or employment class is represented. Its dated regional minimum wages now include DKI Jakarta, West Java, Central Java, Riau,
Bali, Banten and 2026 Kepulauan Riau localities, but do not provide a complete Indonesian
locality or sector register. A valid locality/sector that lacks a rate must refuse rather than
use a lower province floor; the shared lookup does not yet enforce that refusal.

JP wage ceiling: **Rp10,547,400 from 1 Mar 2025** (Dec 2025–Feb 2026 periods) and **Rp11,086,300 from 1 Mar 2026**. The official [Kemnaker wage-law index](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/peraturan?tag%5B%5D=pengupahan)
confirms PP 49/2025 as the second amendment to PP 36/2021. Its arts 35B(2) and 35G(2)
specify a **five-digit KBLI**, more than one medium/large employer in the sector and distinct
work risk as conditions for the governor's sector designation, with the sector floor above the
non-sector floor. The medium/large condition is **not automatically an exclusion of workers at
smaller employers** once a sector floor is designated. The
[signed Bali 2026 decisions](https://disnakeresdm.baliprov.go.id/ump-umk), however, describe
**KBLI letter I plus a hotel-star subgroup** without identifying five-digit codes or the
enterprise/risk qualifications. The operative decree wording and the national criteria must be
reconciled by legal review before sealing an exact selector; do not silently expand letter I to
every business or silently discard an officially published floor. A targeted check of the
[DKI governor index](https://jdih.jakarta.go.id/statistik/Keputusan%20Gubernur),
[West Java index](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/cari_produk/45) and
[Bali labour-office wage index](https://disnakeresdm.baliprov.go.id/ump-umk) on 27 September
2026 found the cited decisions. That index check did not establish continuing legal effect. In
West Java, the [signed Kep.876/2025 of 29 December 2025](https://spsibekasi.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/SK-UMSK-JABAR-2026.pdf)
expressly revoked Kep.863/2025 and replaced its 2026 UMSK schedule. The [official PTUN Bandung
case index, 29/G/2026/PTUN.BDG](https://putusan3.mahkamahagung.go.id/direktori/putusan/zaf185ae2d0699709eda313631373335.html)
records a 22 July 2026 first-instance ruling annulling Kep.876 and ordering a new decision.
Appeal finality and any replacement decision have not been authenticated; West Java 2026 UMSK
selection stays refused.

### DKI Jakarta 2026 sector schedule (signed-decree transcription)

The signed [Kep.33/2026 PDF](https://jdih.jakarta.go.id/dokumenPeraturanDirectory/0031/2026KEPGUB003133.pdf)
dictum KETIGA limits this sector floor to workers with **under one year's service**;
KELIMA prohibits a covered employer from paying below it; KETUJUH applies it **from
1 January 2026**, despite the 20 January signature. KEDUA incorporates the annex. The
following groups transcribe all 61 numbered annex lines (five-digit KBLI, monthly rupiah).
Applied 2026-09-28 (engine defect 6/20): seeded as `work_rules.wages.monthly_by_sector` in both
2026 versions, each row `employment.service_years < 1` plus its annex condition, matched on
`employment_terms.worksite_sector` (the five-digit KBLI) at a worksite in `Provinsi DKI Jakarta`;
a second, independent reading of the signed PDF on 28 September 2026 agreed with every figure.
The floor raises `MINIMUM_WAGE_BELOW` only; Kesehatan's floor stays the UMP (Perpres 82/2018
art.32(2)–(3) as amended by Perpres 64/2020). Owner rule 2026-09-28: the decree is silent on how
an unrecorded employer condition reads; default: a declared company fact
(`umsp_export_oriented`, `umsp_assets_over_1_trillion`, `umsp_astra_group`, `umsp_hotel_star`)
reads false/0 until recorded, the codebase's declared-fact rule; lawful because the employer
records the condition it knows, and the run warns rather than pays. Any narrower condition in the last column is essential, not
merely a label.

The binding floor is the higher of the UMP and every matching row. January arrears for payroll
paid before the 20 January signature remain an operator correction, not an engine rule.

### DKI Jakarta December 2025 sector branch warning

The signed [Kep.145/2025 amendment](https://jdih.jakarta.go.id/dokumenPeraturanDirectory/0031/2025KEPGUB0031145.pdf)
replaces the 2025 UMSP annex and says it applies retroactively from **1 January 2025**
(art I(2), art II(2)); it therefore governs December 2025. The annex has 55 numbered
activity entries across eight sections. The automotive and metal/electronic/machinery sections
describe **products without five-digit KBLI codes**. A code-only match would therefore miss
covered workers or falsely include an entire KBLI when the decree narrows the product.

| Amended annex branch                | December 2025 selector and signed rate examples                                                                                                        | Implemented? | If not, why?                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A, manufacturing (15 entries)       | Garment KBLI 13121/13122/13123, 14301/14303, 14111, 15201: Rp5,531,680 with export and non-UMKM condition; chemical/glass branches include Rp5,504,696 | No           | Full per-entry description, export/company-size proof and dated rate not sealed |
| B, accommodation (1)                | KBLI 55110, four/five-star hotel: Rp5,531,680                                                                                                          | No           | Hotel grade and sector rate absent                                              |
| C, finance (2)                      | Bank KBLI 64121/64122: Rp5,531,680, assets above Rp1 trillion and non-UMKM                                                                             | No           | Asset/company-size condition and rate absent                                    |
| D, food/beverage (5)                | KBLI 10437, 10213/10293, 10520, 10616, 10740: Rp5,407,555                                                                                              | No           | Product-specific activity and rate absent                                       |
| E, pharmaceutical/health (1)        | KBLI 21012: Rp5,407,555, assets above Rp1 trillion                                                                                                     | No           | Asset threshold and sector rate absent                                          |
| F, telecommunications (4)           | KBLI 61921, 61922, 58200, 42205: Rp5,407,555                                                                                                           | No           | Full employer activity and rate absent                                          |
| G, automotive (9)                   | **No KBLI supplied**; specific component/vehicle/heavy-equipment products at Rp5,561,000 or Rp5,625,000                                                | No           | Product and activity discriminator absent; no lawful KBLI-only default          |
| H, metal/electronics/machinery (18) | **No KBLI supplied**; product-specific activities at Rp5,400,000–Rp5,475,000                                                                           | No           | Product and activity discriminator and individual rate mapping absent           |

This is a branch warning, **not** a completed 55-entry transcription. The original signed
[Kep.832/2024](https://jdih.jakarta.go.id/dokumenPeraturanDirectory/0031/2024KEPGUB0031832.pdf)
dictum KETIGA limits the sector floor to **under one year's service**; KEEMPAT requires a
wage structure/scale for longer service. The 2025 amendment art II(1) prohibits reducing
an already-higher wage. These predicates are not in the sealed sector selection either.

The [Kemnaker 2026 instrument index](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/inventarisasi) lists 74
instruments as of this review. Its one law (domestic workers), two government regulations
(BPJS asset management; disability concessions/incentives), two presidential regulations
(ad-hoc-judge compensation; fishing Convention 188), thirteen Permenaker, nine ministerial
circulars and individual ministerial/agency decisions were screened by title. The relevant
outsourcing, inspection, internship, vacancy, holiday, platform-bonus and migrant-worker
items are mapped above or below. Ministry-internal organization, vocational competency
standards, awards and unrelated public-sector compensation have no demonstrated private
employer payroll rule. This is an **issuer-index screen**, not review of all 74 texts or of
local, tax, BPJS and court sources. Separately, an official [Kemnaker announcement](https://www.kemnaker.go.id/news/detail/presiden-prabowo-umumkan-serangkaian-kebijakan-perlindungan-pekerja-pada-may-day-2026)
mentions a transport-platform Perpres 27/2026, but no primary instrument was located in the
Kemnaker index. Do not seal a commission/BPJS formula from the announcement alone.

## Provision and branch register

`Probed` means the listed focused production calculation was executed; it is not saved-input,
output, remittance or legal-scope certification. “Implemented?” means the entire dated obligation
branch, including required saved inputs and outputs, is implemented and independently verified.
A selected amount calculation does not qualify as Yes. Every row below is **No** pending the
stated closure evidence, except ID-122, ID-45 and ID-124 (calculation-only branches proven 2026-09-28) and ID-68 (a sourced scope exclusion, round 6). Every instrument's applicable provisions, exceptions and history still
need reconciliation. `External` means the current seed describes a human obligation, not an
evidenced completion workflow.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Date, applicability, facts and expected result                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Current path and evidence Rates are 1% employee + 2% employer under the dated ceiling: **Rp10,547,400 from 1 Mar 2025** and **Rp11,086,300 from 1 Mar 2026**. The ceiling steps each March by prior-year GDP growth (art 29(3)). | ID-25  | [PP 68/2009](https://jdih.kemenkeu.go.id/dok/pp-68-tahun-2009)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Separation amounts taxed at PP 68/2009 final 0/5/15/25% bands; remuneration type and payment within two-year window matter                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `PPH21_FINAL_SEVERANCE`; ID audit/golden Re-read 2026-09-28 from the signed [PP 68/2009 on JDIH Kemnaker](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/PP_No_68_2009.pdf): the uang pesangon bands are **art.4** (0% to Rp50m, 5% to 100m, 15% to 500m, 25% above; art.5 is the two-band scale for pension benefit, THT and JHT); art.1 angka 4 includes UPMK and UPH; art.2(2) joins parts paid within two calendar years; art.1 angka 3 limits “Pegawai” to a resident individual. Seed (all three versions): the bands now run on the tax year’s earlier severance plus this payment, less what was withheld. Goldens: 15% and 25% bands (650m → 100,000,000; 260m → 26,500,000) and same-year accumulation (40m in February + 70m in March → 4,000,000, not 1,000,000).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29]               | Multiple payment accumulation across recipients; certificate/output reconciliation. Seed fixed 2026-09-28: annual-leave UPH paid on departure was in the TER/annual base, now in the PP 68/2009 final base (art.1 angka 4 names UPH; PP 35/2021 art.40(4)(a)) in all three versions. Fixed 2026-09-28: a **non-resident’s** PESANGON/UPMK count toward PPH26 (20% of gross; art.1 angka 3; UU PPh art.26(1); PMK 168/2023 arts.5(1), 12(9), 14(1)) in all three versions and the shared matrix; golden 30m + 240m → 54,146,400. Round 3 (2026-09-28): (1) art.2(2) cross-year join — fixed 2026-09-28: a part paid in the **second** calendar year joins the first year’s bands (elucidation: “dihitung sebagai satu kesatuan untuk pengenaan pajaknya”). The statutory site now exposes `scheme.last_year.{base,employee,employer}` (this employer’s charges in the preceding tax year), and all three versions run the bands on last year + this year + this payment, less both years’ withholding; golden 40m in December 2025 + 70m in January 2026 → 4,000,000 (was 1,000,000). Ceiling: a rehire’s new severance is joined to an earlier one. (2) art.6(1)–(3), fixed 2026-09-28 — a part paid in the **third** calendar year onward is PPh 21 at the art.17(1)(a) rates (UU 7/2021) on that year’s gross, not final and creditable, 120% without an NPWP. New statutory root `scheme.first_year` (earliest tax year this employer charged a base on the scheme); all three versions route `period.year − scheme.first_year ≥ 2` to `progressive(year_to_date.base + base, art.17) × (no_tax_id ? 1.2 : 1) − year_to_date.employee`, and refuse when the scheme’s `no_tax_id` election is undeclared. Golden: 100m in December 2025, 30m in June 2026, 50m UPMK in March 2027 → 2,500,000, 3,000,000 without an NPWP (elucidation figures; was 1,500,000 joined to 2026 in the final bands). Ceiling: the year gross is this scheme’s severance parts only — other income paid that year is not added (the elucidation assumes none) |

Art.1 angka 2 defines a Pekerja Migran Indonesia as a citizen who works for wages outside Indonesia; art.4(1) places the Pemberi Kerja abroad (a legal entity, a household, or ship work); art.5(d) requires a social-security membership number before departure; art.29(1)–(5) makes PMI social security a BPJS programme regulated by ministerial regulation (Permenaker 4/2023, then P2MI 9/2026 from 28 Aug 2026), whose annex sets fixed stage premiums. Art.49(c) and 61 let a company place its own workers abroad and make it responsible for their protection.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Excluded (sourced)                      | Round 6 disposition: out of this template's scope. A PMI's wage is paid by the foreign Pemberi Kerja (arts.1 angka 2, 4(1)), and the PMI premium is a fixed amount per placement stage paid to BPJS before departure and during placement, not a percentage of a wage this payroll pays; the template has no migrant category, so none is charged or refused. Residual owner decision: an art.61 company that keeps a posted worker on this payroll must still price that worker's P2MI 9/2026 stage premium outside payroll; the domestic JKK/JKM rows are not a substitute for it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

An hourly wage is allowed only for part-time workers, and the agreed hourly rate may not be below **monthly wage ÷ 126** (art 16(1)–(4)); the denominator can be reviewed (art 16(5)–(6)). Daily wage = monthly ÷ **25** on a 6-day week or ÷ **21** on a 5-day week (art 17). Output-paid monthly wage = average of the last 12 months (art 19). No statute sets proration for monthly-salaried joiners or leavers; PK/PP/PKB governs. Recorded default (owner rule 2026-09-28): absent a PK/PP/PKB rule, a joiner, a leaver and an unpaid day are priced on calendar days (`work_rules.proration` CALENDAR_DAYS on every version): monthly ÷ the month's calendar days, e.g. 9,300,000 → 310,000 a day in April, 300,000 in May. Inputs: wage unit, part-time status, weekly day pattern, agreed rate, applicable monthly floor and 12-month output history. Overtime is computed on the monthly wage (art 32(1)). Where the wage includes non-fixed allowances and basic + fixed < 75% of the whole wage, the base is **75% of the whole wage** (art 32(3)–(4)). Daily-paid monthly base = daily × 25 (6-day week) or × 21 (5-day week). Output-paid base = 12-month average, floored at the regional minimum (UMK, else UMP, per the elucidation) (art 33(1)–(3)). An existing equal-or-better overtime scheme under another name continues (art 34). Rest-day/holiday bands (art 31(2)–(3)): 6-day week 1–7h ×2, 8th ×3, 9th–11th ×4; holiday on the short day 1–5h ×2, 6th ×3, 7th–9th ×4; 5-day week 1–8h ×2, 9th ×3, 10th–12th ×4. Without a job-group clause in PK/PP/PKB, overtime must be paid (art 27(5)) A time-based PKWT may last **5 years in total including extensions**, with service counted from the PKWT start (art 8(1)–(3)). A daily contract requires **fewer than 21 working days a month**. Working 21+ days in each of 3 consecutive months or longer converts it to PKWTT by law (art 10(3)–(4)), which changes the severance, THR and tax inputs. Art 11(2) sets the minimum content of a daily contract. A PKWT probation clause is void and the period counts as service (art 12). Registration: online within 3 working days of signing, or in writing at the district office within 7 working days where online is unavailable (art 14(1)–(2)). Compensation = service months ÷ 12 × one month's wage. It is due at PKWT end and at each end before an extension (art 15(4)), requires at least 1 continuous month of service (art 15(3)) and does **not apply to foreign workers** (art 15(5)). Wage base (art 16(2)–(4)): basic + fixed allowances; the clean wage if there are no components; basic only where the wage is basic + non-fixed allowances. A work-based PKWT completed early is priced to the completion date (art 16(5)). Micro/small enterprises set the amount by agreement (art 16(6)). On early termination by either party, compensation covers the period already worked (art 17). JKK/JKM (PP 44 art.19) and JHT (PP 46 art.17) are charged on “Upah sebulan”, defined as upah pokok plus tunjangan tetap; JP (PP 45 art.29(1)) on upah pokok plus tunjangan tetap “pada bulan yang bersangkutan” — in the month concerned. None of the three says whether a part month’s base is the contract’s monthly rate or the wage for the days paid; only PP 45’s wording points to the month’s own wage. Read 2026-09-28 from the signed texts                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Owner rule 2026-09-28 applied as a fix in place in all three versions (the law did not move): JHT, JKK and JKM `assessed_on` is `person.terms.monthly_wage` — the basic plus the contract allowances counting toward the scheme, on the terms in force on the month's last employed day — for a monthly-paid worker; ×25 of the day's wage for a daily-paid one (art.19(3), art.17(3)); the wage paid for the month's hours for an hourly one. JP stays on the wage paid in the month (`BASE + ALLOWANCES + ADHOC − ABSENCE − NO_PAY_LEAVE`). Kesehatan unchanged. `statutory-golden-id` joiner (JHT 240,000/444,000, JKK 64,800, JKM 36,000, JP 60,000/120,000, PPh 21 63,408), raise-on-the-16th (JHT 260,000/481,000 on 13,000,000; PPh 21 482,768), two-unpaid-days (JHT 200,000/370,000; PPh 21 195,813) and 15 June leaver (JHT 200,000/370,000; JP 50,000/100,000) goldens were hand-recomputed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29]               | Decision closed. **Owner rule 2026-09-28:** JHT/JKK/JKM on the contract's monthly rate (“Upah sebulan”, PP 46/2015 art.17(1)–(2), PP 44/2015 art.19(1)–(2)); JP on the wage actually paid in the month (“pada bulan yang bersangkutan”, PP 45/2015 art.29(1)). Law silent on a mid-month rate change: default the rate in force on the month's last employed day; lawful because art.19/17 name a monthly rate and the last day's rate is the rate for the month then reported. Law silent on an hourly wage (art.19(3)–(5) name daily, piece and weather piece only): default the wage paid for the month's hours; lawful because it is the upah pokok actually earned for the month. | ID-162 | [PP 45/2015 arts.28–29](https://www.bpjsketenagakerjaan.go.id/assets/uploads/peraturan/15122015_104556_PP%2045%20Tahun%202015.pdf), [Perpres 82/2018 arts.30, 32](https://jdih.kemenkeu.go.id/api/download/FullText/2018/82TAHUN2018PERPRES.pdf) as amended by Perpres 64/2020 and 59/2024, [PP 46/2015 art.9(2)](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/PP_NOMOR_46_TAHUN2015OK.PDF), [PP 44/2015 art.8(2)](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/PP_NOMOR_44_TAHUN2015OK.PDF)                                                                                                                                     | Joiner after the pay cut-off (audit ID-AUD-1, S7b/S18). Hire 2026-11-25, 124,000,000, DKI, risk I: November is deferred and paid in December as back pay, 6/30 = 24,800,000. Contributions run month by month from the first day of work (PP 46 art.9(2), PP 44 art.8(2)); JP is on the wage “pada bulan yang bersangkutan” (PP 45 art.29(1)) and Kesehatan is a monthly contribution capped per month (Perpres 82/2018 art.30, art.32(1)), so November is its own capped month. Expected: November slip JHT 2,480,000/4,588,000, JKK 297,600, JKM 372,000 (ID-161 monthly rate); December slip JHT/JKK/JKM the same, JP 110,863/221,726 (December) + 110,863/221,726 (November, capped at 11,086,300) = 221,726/443,452, Kesehatan 120,000/480,000 + 120,000/480,000 (capped at 12,000,000) = 240,000/960,000                                                                                                                                                    | Re-verified 2026-09-28: the audit's JP/Kesehatan finding is confirmed (December charged each once on the merged 148,800,000); its JHT/JKK/JKM finding is rejected — the November deferred slip already carries them on the monthly rate. Fix in place in all three versions (a transcription fault, the law did not move): the engine exposes `person.period.arrears` (the deferred month's wage this payslip pays, already inside BASE; `contribution.ts`, `eligibility.ts`, kept through `monthlyAssessment` in `contribute.ts`); the JP and KESEHATAN rules band on the own month (`base − person.period.arrears`) and add the arrears month capped (and, for Kesehatan, floored) on its own. The November slip still charges no JP/Kesehatan (its base is empty), so nothing is charged twice. Golden `statutory-golden-id` “a joiner after the cut-off”                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | No [IN-SYSTEM 2026-09-29]               | Owner rule 2026-09-28: law silent on where a deferred month's JP/Kesehatan is charged; default the payslip that pays that month's wage, priced as its own month; lawful because the contribution is due on that month's wage and the employee share can only be deducted when the wage is paid. Below the ceilings the split is arithmetic-neutral. Still No: a deferred month with no November run on file leaves that month's JHT/JKK/JKM uncharged (they ride only the deferred slip); a deferred month under an earlier sealed version is priced on the paying version's ceiling; saved BPJS reporting output by contribution month                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## Source and coverage review — 2026-09-28

Independent source review of this register. A second pass re-checked each finding before it was applied. Nothing below is certified, and every row stays `No`.

- **Sources updated in place, with amendment history:** the UU 13/2003 ledger row now records MK 168/PUU-XXI/2023's two-year mandate (deadline 31 Oct 2026) and the pending, unenacted replacement bill (watch row ID-105; the press-reported DPR date is not recorded). The PP 36/2021 row now cites the [signed Kemnaker PDF](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/PP362021.pdf) and records that PP 49/2025 (17 Dec 2025) amends arts 2, 4–6, 21, 25–28, 34–35, 35A–35J, 49, 69 and 71 but not arts 15–19. The seed's `jurisdiction_settings.json` `work_rules.encashment` authority now cites the same signed Kemnaker PDF in all three sealed versions (SRC-4, 2026-09-28; was a learning.hukumonline.com copy). The PDF is a scan with no text layer, so arts 15, 16(1) and 17 were checked against the page images (pp. 10–11): the transcription was correct and no value changed; `statutory-golden-id` asserts the citation. The PP 44/2015 chain now includes [PP 82/2019](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/peraturan/detail/1631/peraturan-pemerintah-nomor-82-tahun-2019) and [PP 49/2023](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/2023pp049.pdf) (6 Oct 2023, arts 16A/18A), taken from Kemnaker JDIH because peraturan.bpk.go.id is behind a Cloudflare challenge. The ledger and ID-15 carry both JP ceilings: Rp10,547,400 from 1 Mar 2025 and Rp11,086,300 from 1 Mar 2026. The authority is SE B/1226/022026, and the [BPJS Ketenagakerjaan public statement](https://x.com/BPJSTKinfo/status/2028647882178388161) is corroboration only. The BPJS JP programme page redirects to the homepage and is not citable. ID-15 now states pension age 59 for 2025–2027 and the art 15(4) deferral.
- **New rows:** ID-105 (pending manpower law); ID-106 wage-unit conversions (÷126 part-time hourly floor, ÷25/÷21, 12-month output); ID-107 overtime wage base and rest/holiday bands; ID-108–ID-109 PKWT cap, daily-contract conversion, void probation, registration and compensation exceptions; ID-110 probation; ID-111 notice, resignation and mangkir; ID-112 severance wage basis and UPH contents; ID-113 detained worker; ID-114 micro/small severance by agreement; ID-115 PP 36 paid absences; ID-116 wage-payment protections and the PP 49/2025 art 49(3) priority change; ID-117 late-wage fine figures; ID-118 service charge; ID-119 2026 THR superior amount and no instalments; ID-120 2026 holiday and cuti bersama calendar; ID-121 annual-leave floor; ID-122–ID-123 PPh 21 zakat deduction and natura; ID-124 construction BPJS; ID-125 upah proses (art 157A); ID-126 UU 4/2024 maternity, paternity and lactation; ID-127 open rounding row; ID-128–ID-160 one open locality row for each of the 33 provinces outside DKI, West Java, East Java, Central Java and Bali. The province list comes from knowledge and was not reconciled against a Kemendagri source. No figures were fetched for these provinces.
- **Corrections carried in the new rows:** mangkir (art 36(j)/51) is an employer termination, not a resignation. The PP 36 art 50 25% rule concerns a bankrupt worker's estate. Natura taxation runs from 1 Jul 2023. Miscarriage rest is 1.5 months, and the lactation-room duty is UU 4/2024 art 30. The no-instalment THR rule comes from the 2026 circular. The 2026 decree has 16 holiday entries covering 17 days.
- **Still blocked on 28 Sep 2026:** all 17 jdih.kemenkeu.go.id links timed out (PMK 168/2023, PMK 10/72/105 of 2025, PMK 6/2026, PER-11/PJ/2025, PP 58/2023, PP 68/2009, UU 36/2008, UU KUP, Perpres 82/2018). The 7 peraturan.bpk.go.id links (including Perpres 64/2020 and 59/2024) return a Cloudflare challenge, and the 13 jdih.jabarprov.go.id links return Cloudflare error 1000. peraturan.go.id also timed out. These ledger rows are not re-verified for liveness or currency. The pajak.go.id "PMK 168" PDF is a DJP briefing deck, not the signed PMK. A web search found no amendment to PMK 168/2023 and no fourth amendment to Perpres 82/2018; that is not proof of absence. The mkri.id MK 168 PDF was not re-read.
- **Outside this file:** `STATUTORY_SOURCES.ID` in `src/lib/statutory_sources.ts` omits hosts this register cites: jdih.jatengprov.go.id, files.jdih.jatimprov.go.id, disnakeresdm.baliprov.go.id / cloud-ng.baliprov.go.id, disnakertrans.jabarprov.go.id, www.mkri.id, jdih.bp2mi.go.id, www.kemnaker.go.id and www.jakarta.go.id. It also lacks www.bps.go.id (the JP ceiling's GDP input) and peraturan.go.id (fallback for BPK). Drift research therefore cannot see the provincial decrees, MK 168, P2MI 9/2026 or the GDP input.

### Round 2 closures — 2026-09-28

Each item below was resolved from a primary text, then either seeded with a golden, recorded as a
sourced disposition, or reported as an engine defect. Rows stay `No` unless the whole branch is
proven.

- **JP for foreign workers (ID-15):** no conflict remains. PP 45/2015 has no nationality clause;
  art.3(1) makes participation start at registration and first payment, and BPJS Ketenagakerjaan
  does not register a foreign national for JP (its FAQ, an operator statement). The seed charges a
  foreigner only on a recorded JP registration; a golden proves both branches.
- **PP 68/2009 (ID-25):** re-read from the signed text. The pesangon bands are art.4, not art.5
  (a golden comment said art.5; corrected). Same-year accumulation under art.2(2) is seeded and
  proven, as are the 15% and 25% bands; the second-calendar-year join is proven since the `scheme.last_year` root (fixed 2026-09-28). A non-resident's severance belongs in PPh 26 (art.1 angka 3;
  UU PPh art.26(1); PMK 168/2023 arts.5(1), 12(9), 14(1)): fixed 2026-09-28, PESANGON and UPMK
  count toward PPH26 in all three versions and the shared matrix; golden 30m wage + 240m severance
  → PPh 26 54,146,400, no PP 68/2009 charge.
- **THR no-instalment (ID-13, ID-119):** Permenaker 6/2016 art.5(1) (once a year) and SE
  M/3/HK.04.00/III/2026 item 7 (whole, never in instalments) are seeded as a calendar-year BLOCK
  ceiling on every THR row; a golden proves a half-keyed request pays whole and a second is refused.
- **Final-pay timing (ID-04):** PP 36/2021 art.55(1),(4) and UU 13/2003 art.156(1) set the agreed
  pay date and a one-month interval, not a post-exit deadline. The seed's unsourced 7-day
  `final_pay_due_days` is removed from all three versions; the resigner golden asserts no warning.
- **Social-security base in a part month (new ID-161):** sourced reading only; no instrument
  resolves "Upah sebulan" for a part month. Legal decision needed.
- **Zakat and rounding (ID-122, ID-127):** the signed PMK 168/2023 was read. Zakat paid through the
  employer (art.10(1)(c)) is seeded in the last-period reckoning and proven; PKP rounding down to
  the thousand (art.8(4)) is proven. No other rounding rule exists in PMK 168.
- **UMP 2026 by province (ID-101, ID-128–ID-160):** 13 provinces were read from the signed decree
  or the provincial government's own publication and are seeded as `Provinsi …` keys on both 2026
  versions: Aceh, Sumatera Utara, Kepulauan Riau, Bengkulu, Lampung, Banten, DI Yogyakarta, Bali,
  NTB, Kalimantan Tengah, Kalimantan Selatan, Kalimantan Timur and Gorontalo. DKI Jakarta was
  already seeded. West Java, Central Java and East Java carry no province key, because every city
  and regency there has a UMK (Jabar Kep.862/2025: 27; Jateng Kep.505/2025: 35; Jatim Kep.937/2025:
  38). The other 21 provinces have press leads only and are not seeded. Official hosts refused or
  timed out (JDIH Sumsel and Jatim bot checks, Riau and Kaltara timeouts); rows carry the leads.
  Press lists were wrong in two places (Sumatera Utara 3,228,949 against the official 3,228,971;
  Bengkulu 2,827,250 against the decree's 2,827,250.90). At this pass, no UMK outside DKI and West Java was seeded;
  the later Bali, Banten and Kepulauan Riau locality additions are recorded below.
- **Dec 2025 version at this pass:** 2025 UMPs outside DKI were not seeded, so a province workplace refused in
  December 2025. Later 2025 additions are recorded below.

### Round 3 closures — 2026-09-28

- **UMP by province (ID-128–ID-160):** six more 2026 UMPs are seeded on both 2026 versions, each
  from the signed decree or the provincial government's own publication: Bangka Belitung
  Rp4,035,000 and Sulawesi Selatan Rp3,921,088.79 (signed decrees read in full), Sulawesi Tenggara
  Rp3,306,496.18, Papua Rp4,436,283, Papua Barat Rp3,841,000 and Papua Tengah Rp4,285,848
  (provincial announcements naming the decree). Nineteen 2026 province keys are now seeded, besides DKI Jakarta; 15 remain
  press-only, with the host that refused each recorded in its row (Sumatera Barat, Riau, Jambi,
  Sumatera Selatan, NTT, Kalimantan Barat, Kalimantan Utara, Sulawesi Utara, Sulawesi Tengah,
  Sulawesi Barat, Maluku, Maluku Utara, Papua Barat Daya, Papua Pegunungan, Papua Selatan).
  Papua Pegunungan's decree was reportedly signed in January 2026, so its start date must be read
  from the decree, not assumed.
- **Dec 2025 version:** nine 2025 UMPs are seeded — Bangka Belitung from its signed 2025 decree and Sulawesi
  Tenggara from the provincial announcement naming its 2025 decree; Sumatera Utara, Kepulauan Riau, Banten and Gorontalo from the 2025 figure
  each province states in its own 2026 announcement; Papua Barat and Sulawesi Tengah from 2025
  provincial announcements; Papua from its 2026 announcement's stated rise. Round 2's "deliberate"
  refusal in December 2025 now holds only for the provinces not listed.
- **The Kemnaker UMP compilation is not an authority.** `satudata.kemnaker.go.id` (UMP 2025,
  UMP 2026) was used only to cross-check: its 2026 sheet truncates sen (Bengkulu, Riau, Sulawesi
  Selatan) and gives Sumatera Utara 3,228,949 against the province's 3,228,971
  (2,992,559 × 1.079). No figure was seeded from it alone.
- **ID-25, ID-119:** the cross-year join, art.6 and art.5(2) were engine defects (the statutory site
  held one tax year; the ad hoc band limit could not count the worker's holiday dates). All three
  fixed 2026-09-28, each with its hand-computed `statutory-golden-id` golden.
- **ID-127, ID-161:** each row now states the exact owner decision and its consequence.
- **Stale text fixed:** ID-101 (Bali) said the 2026 UMP was absent while it was seeded; the
  verification section below still reported the round-2 todo and “no seed rule was changed”.

### Round 4 closures — 2026-09-28

Owner rule 2026-09-28: where the law states a rule it is followed exactly; where it is silent, a
lawful, consistent default is recorded in the row as “Owner rule 2026-09-28: law silent; default X;
lawful because Y”.

- **ID-161 (social-security base in a part month):** closed. JHT, JKK and JKM are charged on the
  contract's monthly rate (PP 46/2015 art.17(1)–(2), PP 44/2015 art.19(1)–(2): “Upah sebulan”); JP
  on the wage paid in the month (PP 45/2015 art.29(1): “pada bulan yang bersangkutan”). Fix in
  place in all three versions (a transcription, not a law change). Defaults where the law is
  silent: a mid-month raise takes the rate in force on the month's last employed day; an hourly
  worker is charged on the wage paid for the month's hours. The joiner, raise, unpaid-day and
  leaver goldens were hand-recomputed (figures in ID-161).
- **ID-127 (rounding):** closed. PKP rounding is the only prescribed rule; whole-rupiah half-up
  elsewhere is the default. The minimum-wage comparison is in sen and never rounds a fractional
  floor below itself — golden at Sulawesi Selatan 3,921,088.79 (seeded) and Central Java
  2,327,386.07 (injected; Central Java stays unseeded because every locality has a UMK).
- **ID-62 (piece-rate history):** the legal question is closed by default (average of the months
  available); the piece-rate pay basis itself is not modelled.
- **ID-24 (PPh 26):** regular-wage golden added — 20,000,000 → PPh 26 4,129,600 on gross
  20,648,000; no PPh 21 or JP.
- **UMP retry:** Nusa Tenggara Timur 2025 Rp2,328,969.69 seeded on the December 2025 version from
  the Biro Adpim Setda NTT announcement naming Kep. 430/KEP/HK/2024. The remaining 2026 provinces
  were retried: jdih.sumselprov.go.id holds at a bot check in the in-app browser;
  disnakertrans.sumselprov.go.id, jdih.sultengprov.go.id, diskominfo.kaltaraprov.go.id,
  ppid.malutprov.go.id, malukuprov.go.id, jdih.malukuprov.go.id and the KPPN Ternate
  (djpb.kemenkeu.go.id) notice timed out in curl and were refused by the in-app browser;
  mediacenter.riau.go.id still redirects to its archive; the only Jambi decree PDF found on
  nakertrans.jambiprov.go.id is the 2023 Kota Jambi UMK. The Papua Tengah announcement states no
  2025 figure. Nothing else was seeded; press figures stay leads.
- **SRC-1 (Kesehatan ledger links):** fixed. The Perpres 59/2024 link (BPK Details/284996) opened
  Perbup Kab. Purwakarta 111/2021 and the Perpres 64/2020 link (Details/135059) opened PP 21/2020;
  both now point at the BPK records verified in the in-app browser on 28 Sep 2026 — Details/285181
  (Perpres 59/2024, Perubahan Ketiga atas Perpres 82/2018, ditetapkan 8 Mei 2024) and
  Details/136650 (Perpres 64/2020, Perubahan Kedua). Citation only: no seed figure or version
  changes; the 12,000,000 ceiling and UMP floor goldens stand.
- **UMK/UMSP/UMSK:** reported as an engine defect (ID-03): no per-employment workplace floor and no
  monthly KBLI sector floor with conditions.

### Round 6 closures — 2026-09-28

- **ID-45:** closed as a calculation branch. PP 45/2015 art.6 (signed text) is a pension-benefit
  liability on a benefit event, not a payslip line; art.5(5) collection is what the ASSESS rows do.
  The stale "owner confirmation that JP stays SKIP" is removed (JP is ASSESS since JP-LOCAL).
- **ID-124:** Permenaker 5/2021 art.71(1)–(2) seeded in place in all three versions: JKK 1.74% for
  the PKWT, daily and piece workers (art.65) of an employer with company fact
  `jkk_jasa_konstruksi`; JKM stays 0.30%. The contract-value premium for unknown wages
  (art.71(3)–(4), 72) is a project premium outside payroll. Golden on three versions.
- **ID-68:** excluded from scope with UU 18/2017 arts.1 angka 2, 4(1), 5(d), 29, 49(c), 61 cited;
  an art.61 own-company posting is the residual owner decision.
- **ID-62:** the trailing three- or twelve-month PAID-wage average is implemented and covered by a golden. Piece-rate pay itself remains unmodelled for Indonesia.
- **UMP retry:** Riau 2026 seeded as its 12 UMKs from the Pemprov Riau Media Center archive route
  (`/arsip/94797`, which serves the article the `/read/` URL redirects away from). Sulawesi Utara
  2025 (Rp3,775,425, Pemprov Sulut portal) read but not seeded: a province key would floor its
  UMK localities at the UMP. Retried and still refused: Sumatera Barat (nakertrans SPA returns a
  shell for every file; ppid lists only 2025), Sumatera Selatan (humas, disperin, inspektorat and
  jdih hold at a bot check in the in-app browser), Kalimantan Barat (sudahinakertrans behind a
  Cloudflare challenge; data.kalbarprov.go.id API 403), Kalimantan Utara and Maluku Utara
  (browser navigation refused, curl timed out), Sulawesi Barat and Maluku (curl timed out; no 2026
  page indexed), Jambi, NTT, Papua Barat Daya, Papua Pegunungan and Papua Selatan (no official
  2026 page found). Press and state-broadcaster (RRI) figures stay leads.
- **Seed gap found:** `placeWage` floors a locality with no key of its own at its province key.
  Provinces seeded with a UMP key but an incomplete UMK list (e.g. Sumatera Utara and December
  2025 Kepulauan Riau) can therefore floor a city with a higher UMK at the UMP. Bali, Banten and
  2026 Kepulauan Riau locality rates were subsequently added below. A shared valid-locality guard
  and blocking missing-floor result are still required: deleting a province key merely makes the
  current payroll skip its wage comparison.

### Employed intern wage floor — 2026-09-29

[UU 13/2003 art.22(1)–(3)](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/peraturan_file_13.pdf) requires a written apprenticeship agreement stating rights, duties and duration; without one, the participant becomes a worker of the company. Its explanation distinguishes apprenticeship pocket money from a worker’s wage. The `INTERN` and `APPRENTICE` labels in `employment_terms` do not prove that agreement or nonemployee status. Removed the blanket `INTERN` wage-floor exclusion from all three ID wage tables (`applies_when` empty). This makes the minimum-wage check apply to every recorded employment, including an employed intern. A separately proven apprenticeship stipend needs its own classification and payment path in Phase 2; source evidence and a saved-input/refusal probe remain outstanding. ID-36 stays `No`.

### Locality and sector seed pass — 2026-09-29

The earlier locality-pass notes are superseded by this table. These are data changes in the December 2025 and January/March 2026 seed versions, **not** payroll or saved-run certification. All rates below apply from 1 January of the stated year except the 2025 Jepara UMSK amendment, effective 10 February 2025. Minimum wages apply to workers with less than one year of service; longer-service pay follows the employer's wage structure and scale. The `INTERN` label no longer excludes a recorded employee from the floor: [UU 13/2003 art.22](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/peraturan_file_13.pdf) requires a written apprenticeship agreement and makes a participant without one a worker.

| Lampung 2026          | UMSP KBLI 10434 at Rp3,108,689, for service below one year                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Signed [Kep.865/2025, diktum KEDUA–KETIGA](https://jdih.lampungprov.go.id/product-hukum/provinsi/11560/pdf/penetapan-upah-minimum-provinsi-lampung-dan-upah-minimum-sektoral-provinsi-lampung-tahun-2026).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

**KBLI edition boundary:** [PerBPS 7/2025 arts.5–7](https://jdih.bps.go.id/public/dokumen-hukum/eyJpdiI6IlNWcVBqWnk2aWJ6RnEvK3NndWZma1E9PSIsInZhbHVlIjoiMVFqTVFkMXRCS01HZUp6TG9qVUw0dz09IiwibWFjIjoiOTNiNDVjZmY4ZDdkNzEyOTA1ZjViYTZiNTNjYTc5YWU2MGNiODgxODY0MTc0MDkxOTc0MDZiYTMyM2IyZThjNiIsInRhZyI6IiJ9) took effect on 18 December 2025; [BPS states](https://www.bps.go.id/id/news/2026/04/27/897/bps-rilis-tabel-konversi-klasifikasi-baku-lapangan-usaha-indonesia-kbli-2020-2025-.html) that 2020 and 2025 classifications ran in parallel during the transition. Employment terms record a dated 2020/2025 edition. The three wage versions declare their 2020 edition and 233 verified five-digit KBLI 2025 codes with exactly one KBLI 2020 source, covering 211 of 293 distinct seeded sector or verified-ordinary old codes. Each pair was cross-checked against [BPS's 2020–2025 conversion, Lampiran 5 and 10](https://www.bps.go.id/id/publication/2026/04/22/909d503355d2b7664e43dea8/tabel-konversi-kbli-2020-kbli-2025.html). This includes [OSS-confirmed 62199 → 62019](https://oss.go.id/id/kbli/detail/5e8659f8-01e8-48d9-bceb-4af4015caa73), BPS's 07221 → 07301, and the split of old hotel code 55110 into new 55101–55105; each new hotel code has only 55110 as its old source. Where a hotel sector floor applies, the new code's five- through one-star class must agree with the dated employer star evidence; missing or conflicting evidence refuses. A 2025 code with several 2020 sources (for example, 10739), or no verified mapping, refuses; 82 seeded old codes have no mapped 2025 successor. Absent/unknown editions and 2025 classifications dated before 18 December 2025 also refuse. Saved-run mapping and refusal probes are written, pending the combined gate.

**Banten legacy scope:** [Kep.704/2025 annex VII](https://ppid.bantenprov.go.id/storage/document/kepgub-banten-upah-minimum-sektoral-kabupaten-dan-kota-tahun-2026-202512241555.pdf) prints 62010 “Aktivitas Pemrograman Computer” and 62020 “Aktivitas Konsultasi dan Manajemen Fasilitas Computer” without a KBLI edition; those codes and descriptions appear in [BPS KBLI 2009](https://ppid.bps.go.id/upload/doc/Perka_BPS_No_57_Tahun_2009__Klasifikasi_Baku_Lapangan_Usaha_Indonesia_Thn_2009_Cetakan_III_1659512073.pdf), while BPS's 2015–2020 and 2020–2025 five-digit tables do not establish an exact, singly applicable current-code selector for that wage order. Tangerang Selatan remains strict for 2020 62019, 2025 62199 and the literal 62010/62020 until the order's intended classification and scope are authenticated. A category-level guess could underpay the sector wage.

**Unseeded orders:** Jawa Timur 2025 [Kep.776/2024](https://files.jdih.jatimprov.go.id/jdih-prod/uploads/topics/2024kg00350776.pdf) UMSK was amended by [Kep.804/2024](https://files.jdih.jatimprov.go.id/jdih-prod/uploads/topics/2024kg00350804.pdf) effective 1 January 2025 and [Kep.65/2025](https://files.jdih.jatimprov.go.id/jdih-prod/uploads/topics/2025kg00350065.pdf) effective 3 February 2025. They change KBLI rows and add employer-status conditions (PMA/PMDN Tbk); no reliable complete merged selector has been sealed. Also unseeded: 2025 Jatim UMSP, DIY sector orders, 2025/26 Kepri and Riau UMSP/UMSK, 2025 Banten UMSP/UMSK and unresolved 2026 Banten UMSK locality groups, and DKI 2026 named-job sector rows 58–59 and 61. Source/selector work remains open for the other provinces and their localities.

**Dated strict-sector refusal:** The December 2025 version refuses unmatched KBLI at every named workplace in Banten, Jawa Barat, Jawa Timur, Kepulauan Riau and Riau. The January and March 2026 versions do the same in Jawa Barat, Kepulauan Riau and Riau; their already seeded Banten locality gates remain. These are issued sector orders with unsealed annex selectors or unresolved continuing effect, so ordinary UMK/UMP cannot be asserted there by an unmatched code. Evidence: [Banten's 2025 UMSP Kep.457/2024 announcement](https://biroadpimpro.bantenprov.go.id/berita/pj-gubernur-al-muktabar-tetapkan-ump-banten-2025-naik-menjadi-rp290519990) and [UMSK revision record](https://bantenprov.go.id/berita/pj-gubernur-banten-a-damenta-pimpin-rakor-usulan-revisi-umsk-kabupatenkota-2025), [Jawa Barat 2025 UMSP](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2024kg00320782.pdf) and [amended UMSK](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2024kg00320838.pdf), [Jawa Timur 2025 UMSK amendments](https://files.jdih.jatimprov.go.id/jdih-prod/uploads/topics/2024kg00350804.pdf), [Riau 2025 UMSP](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/89326) and [UMSK](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/89349), [Jawa Barat 2026 UMSP](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2025kg00320860.pdf) and [contested UMSK Kep.876](https://spsibekasi.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/SK-UMSK-JABAR-2026.pdf), [Riau 2026 sector announcement](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/94797), and [Kepri's 2026 announcement naming UMSP/UMSK decisions and predecessor 2025 rates](https://kepriprov.go.id/siaran-pers/832-rilis-diskominfo-xii-2025). Source-backed complete annex selectors or exact ordinary-sector attestations are needed to release these gates. Saved-run refusal probes are written, pending the combined gate.

**West Java provincial sectors:** Signed [2025 Kep.782/2024](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2024kg00320782.pdf) sets KBLI 01270 UMSP at Rp2,201,519.65; the lowest seeded [2025 UMK](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2024kg00320798.pdf) is Banjar Rp2,204,754.48. Signed [2026 Kep.860/2025](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2025kg00320860.pdf) sets twelve construction KBLI at Rp2,339,995 for medium/large employers; the lowest seeded [2026 UMK](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2025kg00320862.pdf) is Pangandaran Rp2,351,250. Neither UMSP raises any locality floor in these complete UMK tables; both years' UMSK selectors still require separate resolution, so the strict gate stays.

**Remaining sector-order evidence:** West Java 2025 [Kep.838/2024](https://jdih.jabarprov.go.id/page/eksekusi_download/32/2024kg00320838.pdf) replaces the full UMSK annex across 18 localities, but several lines identify products or employer types rather than a five-digit KBLI; current facts cannot assert those matches. The 2026 [Kep.876/2025 signed copy](https://spsibekasi.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/SK-UMSK-JABAR-2026.pdf) needs an authenticated post-judgment status. Riau's official [2025 UMSP report](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/89326), [2025 UMSK report](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/89349) and [2026 sector table](https://mediacenter.riau.go.id/arsip/94797) provide amounts and broad sectors without complete signed KBLI/activity annexes. Kepri's [2026 provincial announcement](https://kepriprov.go.id/siaran-pers/832-rilis-diskominfo-xii-2025) names UMSP/UMSK decisions and amounts without their selectors; a 2025 Kep.1415/2024 copy was located only on a secondary host, and the local UMSK schedule is still missing. Banten's official [2025 UMSP announcement](https://biroadpimpro.bantenprov.go.id/berita/pj-gubernur-al-muktabar-tetapkan-ump-banten-2025-naik-menjadi-rp290519990) and [UMSK revision record](https://bantenprov.go.id/berita/pj-gubernur-banten-a-damenta-pimpin-rakor-usulan-revisi-umsk-kabupatenkota-2025) do not supply a complete operative annex. Jawa Timur's three signed 2025 UMSK decisions above require a merged dated selector including PMA/PMDN Tbk facts. No partial provincial row can release a locality from strict refusal while a higher local UMSK remains unresolved. The saved refusal probes include West Java's officially named 01270 and 41011 codes; neither can bypass the local boundary.

**Long-service and output wage boundary:** [PP 36/2021 arts.18–20 and 24](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/PP362021.pdf) makes the under-one-year minimum wage distinct from the employer's structure and scale for a worker with at least one year of service. [PP 49/2025 art.21](https://jdih.kemnaker.go.id/asset/data_puu/2025pp0049.pdf) requires the employer to apply the scale and notify the worker of their own grade. The saved term now records the grade, its basic-wage minimum, scale effective date, individual notice date, company reference and the scale/individual notice file. The run refuses absent, future-dated or below-grade evidence for service of at least one year, in addition to the existing protective UMP/UMK check pending the unresolved PP 36 art.24 interpretation. This per-worker record does not independently prove that the same company scale is internally consistent across all grades or that the employer delivered the notice. Under PP 36 arts.18–19, pure result-only wages need an agreed unit/rate and the last twelve paid months for a statutory monthly wage; that complete wage-floor path is not sealed. Zero-basic PIECE_RATE/TASK_BASIS terms explicitly refuse before a misleading monthly-basic-versus-UMP comparison. Workday piece units and rates exist, and the separate BPJS JHT/JKK/JKM three- or twelve-month paid-wage average is implemented and saved-probed, but neither supplies the PP 36 wage valuation. Scale and pure-output saved-run probes are written, pending the combined gate. [Perpres 59/2024 art.32(4)–(5)](https://peraturan.bpk.go.id/Download/344279/Perpres%20Nomor%2059%20Tahun%202024.pdf) independently exempts micro/small PPU from the ordinary BPJS Kesehatan UMK/UMP contribution floor: all three versions refuse their Kesehatan price until the separate actuarial floor is published, while the ordinary employer branch continues to use UMK/UMP.

**BPJS Kesehatan nonmonthly boundary:** [Perpres 82/2018 art.33](https://jdih.kemenkeu.go.id/FullText/2018/82TAHUN2018PERPRES.pdf) defines the PPU contribution wage by basic pay and fixed allowances, and [Perpres 59/2024 art.32](https://peraturan.bpk.go.id/Download/344279/Perpres%20Nomor%2059%20Tahun%202024.pdf) states a _monthly_ UMP/UMK floor and Rp12m ceiling. Neither source seals the conversion of a DAILY or HOURLY contract rate into the Kesehatan monthly contribution wage. The current saved engine had used that raw rate as its monthly denominator, which could overprice the charge. Eligible DAILY/HOURLY PPU saved runs now refuse before pricing; the MONTHLY path remains available. The daily minimum-wage divisor and hourly part-time floor are probed at their own rule boundary, but eligible nonmonthly terms lack end-to-end payroll coverage until an authoritative Kesehatan monthly basis is implemented. A foreign worker outside the six-month Kesehatan participation condition remains separately assessable for other schemes.

**Foreign short-contract history boundary:** [Perpres 82/2018 art.1(2), as restated in Perpres 59/2024](https://peraturan.bpk.go.id/Download/344279/Perpres%20Nomor%2059%20Tahun%202024.pdf) measures at least six months of _work in Indonesia_, not just the length of the current contract. The prior seed skipped Kesehatan for every current foreign fixed-term contract under six months, even if earlier Indonesian work was unknown. Such a saved run now needs an explicit `NONE` prior-work declaration, a review date from the current employment start through the assessment date, and a source reference on each dated term in the pay window. Unknown or any prior work refuses until the cumulative work history and participation date are resolved; it cannot silently produce zero. The positive short-contract and missing/prior/future/no-reference saved probes are written, pending the combined gate. The broader foreign participation history for JHT/JKK/JKM is still an open analogous gap.

**Current selection boundary:** ID's workplace-keyed wage path requires an exact locality or an allowed province-only workplace; an unmatched city/regency cannot inherit UMP. A covered employee's missing wage version, place, KBLI edition or sealed sector conversion refuses, and the dated strict-sector places above reject unverified ordinary-floor selection. A missing hotel class or other applicable decree fact refuses where it changes the sector floor; schema defaults alone are not evidence. The explicit UMP-valued locality keys above preserve lawful provincial rates only for verified localities. BPJS Kesehatan still uses UMK or UMP rather than a sectoral floor. Saved-path probes are written; the combined gate remains pending, and unseeded local/sector orders keep ID minimum-wage coverage open.

### Round 7 triage — 2026-09-29

Law re-checked for each item below; nothing sealed without a primary text. Owner rule 2026-09-28 applies.

- **Rejected — ID-70 (JKK recomposition bill):** see the ID-70 row. Permenaker 3/2025 art.14(3)–(4) caps the 0.14% recomposition base only; the JKK bill is the whole group rate on the whole wage. Golden added (group II, 25,000,000 → 135,000).
- **Register corrected — ID-137 (Lampung), ID-139 (DI Yogyakarta):** their cells still said no UMK was seeded; the locality pass had seeded them. Values re-read (Bandar Lampung signed order; DIY Gunungkidul announcement) and proved by a new golden on all three versions.

- **Seed-bank data, outside this lane — ID-51, ID-120 (holidays, cuti bersama):** holidays are `jurisdiction_holidays` rows that live in the seed bank, not in `seed/jurisdiction/ID/`; employer cuti-bersama adoption is a saved company input.
- **Shared code needed (no seed or golden can close them):** worker/recipient taxonomy and domestic-worker, apprenticeship, government-assistance, internship, BPU and platform branches (ID-01, 22, 31, 34, 36, 57, 58, 60); locality admission/refusal guard, micro/small, wage-structure, sector-designation, DKI UMSP job-type and new-province selectors (ID-02, 03, 53, 54, 71, 89, 94–104, 128–158 residuals); consent, schedules, attendance and OT variants (ID-06, 08, 09, 10, 43, 107); leave history, events and balances (ID-11, 12, 37, 38, 115, 121, 126); THR history, deadline, late penalty and superior scheme (ID-13, 76, 119); piece/output and daily wage history (ID-14, 17, 35, 62, 72); BPJS registration history, relief, JKP branches and deferred months (ID-16, 18, 64, 69, 86, 88, 90, 91, 162); PPh 21/26 year-to-date, DTP, treaty, natura, residency and accumulation (ID-21, 23, 24, 25, 73, 74, 75, 84, 85); PKWT, termination, severance, detention, insolvency, court/inspector awards and late-wage fine (ID-27, 28, 32, 39, 44, 50, 61, 108–114, 116, 117, 125); workforce duties (ID-07, 42, 47, 48, 49, 59, 77, 106). Each needs a saved input, a selector or a refusal the shared engine does not offer; the rows keep their stated closure evidence.
- **Retry needed, primary source unreached:** the remaining UMK/UMSK schedules in ID-79–83 second reads, ID-132, 138, 142 residuals and the UMP-only provinces (ID-128, 129, 134, 136, 140, 143–145, 148, 151, 152, 155, 156, 158) were not re-attempted beyond round 6's recorded failures.
